) is p.s.d. symmetric, to establish the result in
Proposition

2.8
, it suffices to bound the spectral norm

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{0}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|,

(22)

for
\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{0},\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{1},\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}
defined in (
21
).

Without loss of generality, assume that the events
\zeta_{1}
and
\zeta_{2}
are independent of
\mathbf{x}_{s}
, and set
\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}=\zeta_{1}\bigcap\zeta_{2}
as well as
\delta_{3}=\Pr(\neg\zeta_{3})
.
To bound (
22
), we first recall the following results from the proof of
Dereziński et al. [2021a]
,
Dereziński et al. [2021b]
.

Lemma C.2
.

Under the settings and notations of
Proposition

2.8
, we have,

1.

for a p.s.d. random matrix
\mathbf{M}
(or a non-negative random variable) living in the probability space of
\mathbf{S}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{M}]=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[(\prod^{3}_{j=1}\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{j}})\mathbf{M}]}{\Pr(\zeta)}\preceq\frac{1}{1-\delta}{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{1}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\mathbf{M}]\preceq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{M}],

(23)

where
\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{j}}
is the indicator of the event
\zeta_{j}
.

2.

\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|=O(\frac{1}{m})
.

Next, we bound the terms invoking
\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{0}
and
\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}
in (
22
), particularly by emphasizing the difference between our proof of
Proposition

2.8
here from that of
Dereziński et al. [2021a]
,
Dereziński et al. [2021b]
.
By
\delta_{3}\leq\frac{1}{2}
and

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{0}=-\frac{1}{1-\delta_{3}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}(\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}-\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A})\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}],

it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{0}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq 12{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[(1+\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}].

We now need to bound the (conditional) expectation
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[(1+\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}]
.
To do this, we resort to
Condition

C.1
with an appropriate choice of
\alpha
.
Note that

{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}]=\tr(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A})=d_{\mathrm{eff}},

per
Definition

2.2
, it follows that

\displaystyle\Var[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}]

\displaystyle=\Var[\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s}}/\sqrt{\pi_{i_{s}}}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i_{s}}/\sqrt{\pi_{i_{s}}}]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}[(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{2}]-({\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}])^{2}=\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{(_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{i})^{2}}{\pi_{i}}-d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}-d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}=O(\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}),

for max importance sampling approximation factor
\rho_{\max}
in
Definition

2.3
, which implies that
\alpha
in
Condition

C.1
satisfies

\alpha=O(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}).

Further, by Chebyshev’s inequality, one has, for
x\geq 2d_{\mathrm{eff}}
, that
\Pr(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\geq x~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime}})\leq\alpha d_{\mathrm{eff}}/x^{2}
.
This further leads to, for
\delta_{3}\leq 1/m^{3}
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{0}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 12\int^{\infty}_{0}\Pr(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})dx+O\Big(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 24m^{2}\delta_{3}+12\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}}\Pr(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})dx+O\Big(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{m}\Big)+\frac{\alpha d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}=O\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{eff}}}}{m}\right).

We then move on to bound the last term concerning
\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}
in (
22
).
Applying Cauchy-Schwarz inequality twice, we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[(\tilde{\gamma}_{s}-\gamma)^{2}\right]}}_{\tilde{T}_{1}}\cdot\underbrace{\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}\sqrt[4]{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{4}\right]}}_{\tilde{T}_{2}}\cdot\underbrace{\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}\sqrt[4]{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{4}\right]}}_{\tilde{T}_{3}}.

(24)

We start by bounding the term
\tilde{T}_{3}
using
Condition

C.1
with
\mathbf{B}=\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\mathbf{A}^{\top}
. Noting that
\tr(\mathbf{B})=\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\mathbf{u}\leq 1
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{4}\right]=2{\mathbb{E}}[(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{2}]

\displaystyle=2\Var_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{2}]+2({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s}])^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{(_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{i})^{2}}{\pi_{i}}+2(\tr(\mathbf{B}))^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}+2(\tr(\mathbf{B}))^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}\tr(\mathbf{B})+2(\tr(\mathbf{B}))^{2}\leq 2(\rho d_{\mathrm{eff}}+1)=O(\alpha+1),

for
\alpha=O(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})
in
Condition

C.1
.
This results in
\tilde{T}_{3}=O(\sqrt[4]{\alpha+1})
.
Similarly, we bound
\tilde{T}_{2}
by taking
\mathbf{B}=\mathbf{A}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top}
in
Condition

C.1
.
It follows
\tr(\mathbf{B})\leq\mathbf{u}^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2})^{2}\mathbf{u}\leq 6^{2}
and
\tr(\mathbf{B}^{2})\leq 6^{4}
, so that
\tilde{T}_{2}=O(\sqrt[4]{\alpha+1})
.

It thus remains to bound the first term
\tilde{T}_{1}
in (
24
).
Noting
\bar{\gamma}={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\tilde{\gamma}_{s}]=1+\frac{\gamma}{m}\tr({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A})
, we write

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[(\tilde{\gamma}_{s}-\gamma)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2(\gamma-\bar{\gamma})^{2}+\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[(\tr(\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}])\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A})^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[(\tr(\mathbf{A}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top})-\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{2}].

Analogously as above, letting
\mathbf{B}=\mathbf{A}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top}
so that
\tr(\mathbf{B}^{2})\leq 36d_{\mathrm{eff}}
in
Condition

C.1
, we have, for
m\geq 2d_{\mathrm{eff}}
that

\displaystyle\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\tr(\mathbf{A}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top})-\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\right)^{2}\right]=\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\pi_{i}(\tr(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top})-\frac{{}_{i}^{\top}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i}}{\pi_{i}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\tr(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top})\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{(_{i}^{\top}\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i})^{2}}{\pi_{i}}\right]-\frac{4\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\tr(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{72\gamma^{2}\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}-\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\tr(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{72\gamma^{2}\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}\leq\frac{288\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}=O\left(\frac{\alpha d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}\right),

with again
\alpha=O(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})
in
Condition

C.1
.

This, following the line of arguments in
Dereziński et al. [2021a]
, further leads to

{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[(\tilde{\gamma}_{s}-\bar{\gamma})^{2}\right]\leq O\left(\frac{\alpha d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}\right),

which, together with
|\gamma-\bar{\gamma}|=O(\sqrt{\alpha d_{\mathrm{eff}}}/m)
, yields that
\tilde{T}_{1}=O(\sqrt{\alpha d_{\mathrm{eff}}}/m)
.

This allows us to conclude that

\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq\tilde{T}_{1}\cdot\tilde{T}_{2}\cdot\tilde{T}_{3}=O\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{eff}}}}{m}\right).

Putting everything together, we conclude that

\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\tilde{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{eff}}}}{m}\right),

with
\alpha=O(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})
in
Condition

C.1
.
This concludes the proof of
Proposition

2.8
.

\Box

Appendix D
Proof and Discussions of
Theorem

3.1

In this section, we start by presenting in
Section

D.1
the intuition for the self-consistent equation in (
4
) of
Theorem

3.1
.
The detailed proof of
Theorem

3.1
is given in
Section

D.2
.
In
Section

D.3
, we provide discussions and auxiliary results on
Theorem

3.1
.

D.1
RMT Intuition on the Self-consistent Equation in
Theorem

3.1

Here, we present a heuristic derivation of the self-consistent equation in (
4
) of
Theorem

3.1
.
Let us recall some notations from
Theorem

3.1
.
Let
\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}=\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s}}/\sqrt{\pi_{i_{s}}}\mathbf{A}
as in
Appendix

C
.
For the ease of further use, we denote

\mathbf{Q}=(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}=\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum^{m}_{s=1}\mathbf{x}_{s}{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}_{s}+\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}.

and
\mathbf{Q}_{-s}=(\sum_{j\neq s}\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{x}_{j}{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}_{j}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
, for which we get

\sum^{m}_{s=1}\frac{1}{m}{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}_{s}]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}=\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}.

Then, we follow the
deterministic equivalent
framework (see
Couillet and Liao [2022, Chapter 2]
for an introduction) and show that
\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{Q}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\|\simeq 0
, for
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}=\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C}
, where
\mathbf{D}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n}
is the diagonal matrix in
Theorem

3.1
.
Note that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{Q}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\|=\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{Q}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}-{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\|\simeq 0,

and using Sherman-Morrison formula in
Lemma

A.3
, we further ascertain that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}]

\displaystyle=\sum^{m}_{s=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}}{1+\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}/m}\right]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}}{1+_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}}{1+_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\right].

Using the rank-one perturbation lemma in
Silverstein and Bai [1995, Lemma 2.6]
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}]

\displaystyle\simeq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{Q}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}}{1+_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{Q}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}}{1+_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{Q}\right]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1},

so that
\mathbf{D}=\diag\left\{\frac{m\pi_{i}}{m\pi_{i}+_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}
.
This leads to the self-consistent equation in (
4
) of
Theorem

3.1
.

D.2
Detailed Proof of
Theorem

3.1

As outlined in
Appendix

C
, the proof of
Theorem

3.1
also comes in the following two steps:

1.

construct a high probability event
\zeta
as in (
20
); and

2.

conditioned on that event
\zeta
, bound the spectral norm
\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}\|
using “leave-one-out” analysis.

Furthermore, for each
s\in\{1,\ldots,m\}
, there also exists an index
j=j(s)\in\{1,2,3\}
such that, conditioned on
\zeta_{j}
, we have
\mathbf{Q}\preceq\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\preceq 6\mathbf{H}^{-1}
.

To complete the proof of
Theorem

3.1
, we first rewrite

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|.

Taking
\gamma_{s}=1+\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}
,
s=1,\ldots,m
, and
\tilde{D}_{s}=\frac{1}{1+\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}}
, we then get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}-\mathbf{I}_{d}

\displaystyle=({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\mathbf{Q}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\mathbf{Q}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}}_{\mathbf{Z}_{1}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})]}_{\mathbf{Z}_{2}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\tilde{D}_{s}-\frac{1}{\gamma_{s}})\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}]}_{\mathbf{Z}_{3}},

which yields

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{2}+\mathbf{Z}_{3})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|.

(25)

Then, we bound the first term
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1/2}\|
. Together with the fact that the event
\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}
is independent of
\mathbf{x}_{s}
, and using the Sherman-Morrison formula in
Lemma

A.3
and (
23
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}-\mathbf{Q}]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\frac{1}{m\gamma_{s}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\preceq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\frac{1}{m\gamma_{s}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\preceq\ \frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{}_{j}{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\preceq\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}],

which, by incorporating the fact that
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}\preceq\mathbf{H}
and the event
\zeta
indicates
\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\preceq 6\mathbf{I}_{d}
, leads to

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq\frac{2}{m}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{72}{m}.

(26)

Further, we move on to bound the second term in (
25
). Recalling the assumption regarding
\zeta_{1}
,
\zeta_{2}
,
\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}
and
\delta_{3}
from
Appendix

C
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{3}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}])

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{1-\delta_{3}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}].

Then, it follows that

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 12\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|]

\displaystyle\leq 12{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|]

\displaystyle\leq 12{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[(1+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|)\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}]

\displaystyle\leq 12{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[(1+\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}]=12\delta_{3}+12{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}].

It follows from
Lemma

D.3
and
m\geq 2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{m+2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|=\frac{m+2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}\leq 2,

(27)

so that

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[(\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{2}]=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\pi_{j}\left(\frac{m_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}+_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\frac{D^{2}_{jj}(_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j})^{2}}{\pi_{j}}\leq\sum^{n}_{j=1}\frac{\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j})^{2}}{\pi_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}\leq 4\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}]

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\frac{m\pi_{j}}{m\pi_{j}+_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}\leq\sum^{n}_{j=1}\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}\leq 2d_{\mathrm{eff}}.

Using Chebyshev’s inequality, we get, for
x>2d_{\mathrm{eff}}
,

\displaystyle\Pr(\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})\leq\frac{4\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{x^{2}}.

This, together with
\delta_{3}\leq\delta\leq\frac{1}{m^{3}}
and
m>\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}
further leads to

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\Big)+12\int^{\infty}_{0}\Pr(\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})dx

\displaystyle\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\Big)+24m^{2}\delta_{3}+12\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}}\Pr(\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})dx

\displaystyle\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\Big)+\frac{24}{m}+48\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{x^{2}}dx\leq O\Big(\frac{1}{m}\Big)+\frac{24\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Using (
23
) and (
27
), we bound the last term in (
25
) as follows:

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1,\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[|\tilde{D}_{s}-\gamma_{s}^{-1}||\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1,\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\tilde{D}_{s}-\gamma_{s}^{-1}||\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}|\right]

\displaystyle=\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1,\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}-\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}}{\tilde{D}^{-1}_{s}\gamma_{s}}\right|\left|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1,\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-_{i_{s}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}+\mathbf{v}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v})\right]

\displaystyle=\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\sum^{n}_{j=1}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\right]

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\sum^{n}_{j=1}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\mathbf{v}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{j}{}_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\sum^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\right]

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\sum^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{v}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{j}{}_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{u}\right]

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\mathbf{v}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}38\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\right]\leq\underbrace{38{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\right]}_{M_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}38\frac{|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}|}{m\pi_{j}}}_{M_{2}},

(28)

where in
(a)
we use the fact that
\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\preceq\mathbf{H}
,
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}\preceq\mathbf{H}
and the event
\zeta
indicates
\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\preceq 6\mathbf{I}_{d}
, along with (
27
).Then, by
Lemma

D.1
, we bound the term
M_{1}
in (
28
) as

\displaystyle M_{1}\leq O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Now, it remains to bound the second term
M_{2}
in (
28
).
Using again (
27
), we get

\displaystyle|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]-_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}|=|_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}_{j}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq 2_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}(({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta})[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}-\mathbf{Q}]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}\left(\underbrace{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta})[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|}_{G_{1}}+\underbrace{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}-\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|}_{G_{2}}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\right).

(29)

Noting
\delta_{3}<\frac{1}{m^{3}}
and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{3}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]-\delta_{3}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}|\neg\delta_{3}]),

we have, for the term
G_{1}
defined in (
29
), that

\displaystyle G_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta_{3}}{1-\delta_{3}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}|\neg\delta_{3}])\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{3}(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}|\neg\delta_{3}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|)\leq 24\delta_{3}\leq\frac{24}{m^{3}}.

For the term
G_{2}
in (
29
), it follows from (
26
) that
G_{2}=O(\frac{1}{m})
.
We thus have

\displaystyle M_{2}\leq 76\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\left(O\left(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{m}\right)+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{76\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}\left(O\left(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{m}\right)+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{76\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}\left(O\left(\frac{1}{m^{3}}\right))+O\left(\frac{1}{m}\right)+O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right)+M_{2}\right),

which, for
m>76\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}
, yields that

\displaystyle M_{2}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}\frac{76^{2}\rho_{\max}^{2}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{(m-76\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2}}}\right)=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Putting everything together, we conclude that

\displaystyle\Big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}]\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\mathbf{I}_{d}\Big\|=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

This concludes the proof of
Theorem

3.1
.

\Box

D.3
Discussions on
Theorem

3.1
and Auxiliary Results

Lemma D.1
.

Under the settings and notations of
Theorem

3.1
, then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\right]=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Proof.

Using
\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}z_{i}\leq\left(\sum^{n}_{i=1}z_{i}^{s}\right)^{1/s}
with any variable
z_{i}>0
, along with Jensen’s inequality, we get, for
q\geq 2

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\frac{|_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]|}{m\pi_{j}}\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{n}_{j=1}\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|^{q}\right)^{1/q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|^{q}\right]\right)^{1/q}.

Now, it suffices to bound each term
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|^{q}\right]
.
Use
\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}
to denote the matrix
(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}_{-sl}^{\top}\mathbf{S}_{-sl}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
where
\mathbf{S}_{-sl}
is the matrix
\mathbf{S}
without the
s^{th}
and
l^{th}
rows; let that, for each pair
s
,
l
, one of
\zeta_{1}
,
\zeta_{2}
is independent of both
\mathbf{x}_{s}
and
\mathbf{x}_{l}
. Without loss of generality, we assume that it is
\zeta_{1}
.
Also, let
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}[\cdot]
be the expectation conditioned on
\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}
and the
\sigma
-field
\mathcal{F}_{l}
generated by the basic events
\mathbf{x}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}_{l}.

To apply the Martingale Rosenthal inequality in
Lemma

A.4
, we rewrite
{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]
as the martingale difference sequence

\displaystyle\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},m}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},0}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}=\sum^{m}_{l=1}(Z_{l}-Z_{l-1})

\displaystyle=\sum^{m}_{l=1}\frac{(({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1})[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}+\frac{({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1})[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}])}{m\pi_{j}}

\displaystyle=-\sum^{m}_{l=1}(\phi_{l}+\varphi_{l}),

where

\displaystyle\phi_{l}

\displaystyle=\frac{({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1})[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}

\displaystyle\varphi_{l}

\displaystyle=-\frac{({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1})[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}.

Following
Lemma

A.4
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|^{q}\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|-\sum^{m}_{l=1}(\phi_{l}+\varphi_{l})\right|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{q}^{q}\left(\frac{q}{\log q}\right)^{q}\left(\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[(\phi_{l}+\varphi_{l})^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right]}_{R_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{l=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[|\phi_{l}+\varphi_{l}|^{q}]}_{R_{2}}\right).

In the following, we first bound the term
R_{2}
. Using
(a+b)^{s}\leq 2^{s-1}(a^{s}+b^{s})
with
a>0,~b>0
, gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[|\phi_{l}+\varphi_{l}|^{q}]\leq 2^{q-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\phi_{l}|^{q}\right]+2^{q-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\varphi_{l}|^{q}\right]

which leads to

\displaystyle R_{2}\leq 2^{q-1}\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\phi_{l}|^{q}\right]+2^{q-1}\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\varphi_{l}|^{q}\right].

Next, we bound the term
\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\phi_{l}|^{q}\right]
.
We rewrite

{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}=\frac{1}{m}\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{1+\frac{1}{m}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}}\leq\frac{1}{m}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}.

(30)

Lemma D.2
.

Under the settings and notations of
Theorem

3.1
, we have, for a p.s.d. random matrix
\mathbf{M}
(or a non-negative random variable) living in the probability space of
\mathbf{S}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{M}]=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[(\prod^{2}_{j=1}\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{j}})\mathbf{M}]}{\Pr(\zeta^{\prime})}\preceq\frac{1}{1-\delta}{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{1}}\mathbf{M}]\preceq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}[\mathbf{M}],

(31)

where
\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{j}}
is the indicator of the event
\zeta_{j}
.

Then, together with the Jensen’s inequality, we have, for
q\geq 2
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[|\phi_{l}|^{q}]\leq 2^{q-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right]

\displaystyle+2^{q-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right]+2^{q-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right]

\displaystyle=2^{q}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right]\overset{(a)}{\leq}2^{q+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}\left[\left(\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right],

where in
(a)
we apply
Lemma

D.2
.
Combining

\displaystyle\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}\pi_{i}}\leq\frac{6^{2}\rho_{\max}^{2}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}},

and
\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\preceq 6\mathbf{H}^{-1}
,
it follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}\left[\left(\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right)^{q}\right]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}\left[\pi_{i}\left(\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}\pi_{i}}\right)^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(6\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-2}}{m^{2q-2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}\left[\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(6\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-2}}{m^{2q-2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}\left[\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}\right]\leq\frac{36(6\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-2}}{m^{2q-2}}\cdot\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6^{2q}(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-1}}{m^{2q}}.

(32)

This implies

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[|\phi_{l}|^{q}]\leq 2^{q+1}\cdot\frac{6^{2q}(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-1}}{m^{2q-1}}=\frac{2^{3q+1}\cdot 3^{2q}(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-1}}{m^{2q-1}}.

Subsequently, we proceed to bound the term
|\varphi_{l}|
.
Considering that
\zeta_{1}
is independent of
\mathbf{x}_{l}
, it follows that
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l-1}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]
.
Then, noting that, for
\delta_{2}=\Pr(\neg\zeta_{2})<\frac{1}{m}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]-\frac{\delta_{2}}{1-\delta_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}|\neg\zeta_{2}],

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l-1}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]-\frac{\delta_{2}}{1-\delta_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l-1}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}|\neg\zeta_{2}],

so that

\displaystyle|\varphi_{l}|

\displaystyle=\frac{|({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1})[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}]|}{m\pi_{j}}\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{1-\delta_{2}}\cdot\frac{|({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l-1})[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}|\neg\zeta_{2}]}{m\pi_{j}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\delta_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}|\neg\zeta_{2}]}{m\pi_{j}}\leq\frac{12\delta_{2}{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}<\frac{12\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2}},

(33)

which yields

\displaystyle\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[|\varphi_{l}|^{q}\right]\leq\left(\frac{12\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2-1/q}}\right)^{q}.

We thus get

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q-1}\cdot\frac{2^{3q+1}\cdot 3^{2q}(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2q-1}}{m^{2q-1}}+2^{q-1}\cdot\left(\frac{12\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2-1/q}}\right)^{q}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4q}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2-1/q}}{m^{2-1/q}}\right)^{q}+2^{3q-1}\cdot 3^{q}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{2-1/q}}\right)^{q}\leq 2^{4q+1}\cdot 3^{2q}\left(\frac{(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2-1/q}}{m^{2-1/q}}\right)^{q}.

Now, it remains to bound the first term
R_{1}
. Considering

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[(\phi_{l}+\varphi_{l})^{2}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\phi_{l}^{2}]+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\varphi_{l}^{2}],

and taking
(a+b)^{s}\leq 2^{s-1}(a^{s}+b^{s})
with
a>0,~b>0
again,
we first rewrite

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q/2-1}\cdot 2^{q/2}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\varphi_{l}^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right]+2^{q/2-1}\cdot 2^{q/2}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\phi_{l}^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle=2^{q-1}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\varphi_{l}^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right]+2^{q-1}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\phi_{l}^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right].

Using (
33
) again, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\varphi_{l}^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right]\leq\left(\frac{12^{2}\rho_{\max}^{2}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{2}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}.

Recalling (
30
) and (
32
),
we further derive

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\phi_{l}^{2}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}\left[\frac{({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}])^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}}\right]+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}\left[\frac{({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j}])^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}\left[\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j})^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}}\right]+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}\left[\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j})^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}}\right]

\displaystyle=4{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}\left[\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j})^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}}\right]\leq 8{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l-1}\left[\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j})^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}}\right]

\displaystyle=8\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{1},l-1}\left[\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sl}{}_{j})^{2}}{m^{4}\pi^{2}_{j}\pi_{i}}\right]\leq 8\cdot\frac{6^{4}\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{4}}=\frac{2^{7}\cdot 3^{4}\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{4}},

where in
(a)
, we use the Jensen’s inequality.
We thus attain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\sum^{m}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}},l-1}[\phi_{l}^{2}]\right)^{q/2}\right]\leq\left(\frac{2^{7}\cdot 3^{4}\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}.

This further results in

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q-1}\cdot\left(\frac{12^{2}\rho_{\max}^{2}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}+2^{q-1}\cdot\left(\frac{2^{7}\cdot 3^{4}\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot 2^{q-1}\cdot 2^{7q/2}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}=2^{9q/2}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}.

Putting the above together, for
2-1/q\geq 3/2
, we conclude
that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|^{q}\right]\leq K_{q}^{q}\left(\frac{q}{\log q}\right)^{q}2^{9q/2}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}

\displaystyle+K_{q}^{q}\left(\frac{q}{\log q}\right)^{q}2^{4q+1}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{(\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}})^{2-1/q}}{m^{2-1/q}}\right)^{q}\leq K_{q}^{q}\left(\frac{q}{\log q}\right)^{q}2^{9q/2+2}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}.

Letting
q=\log n
such that
n^{1/q}=O(1)
, for
K_{q}
a constant, we further obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\frac{|_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}{}_{j}]|}{m\pi_{j}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\left(nK_{q}^{q}\left(\frac{q}{\log q}\right)^{q}2^{9q/2+2}\cdot 3^{2q}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)^{q/2}\right)}^{1/q}

\displaystyle=9\cdot 2^{9/2}K_{q}\cdot(4n)^{1/q}\cdot\frac{q}{\log q}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}}=O\left(\frac{q}{\log q}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d_{\mathrm{eff}}^{3}}{m^{3}}}\right).

We thus complete the proof.
∎

Below is the proof of the result in
Footnote

2
.

Lemma D.3

(On the self-consistent
\mathbf{D}
)
.

For a given matrix
\mathbf{A}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times d}
,
let
\mathbf{S}
be a random sampling matrix with number of trials
m
and importance sampling distribution
\{\pi_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
as in
Definition

2.1
, and let
\mathbf{C}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times d}
be a p.s.d. matrix and
d_{\mathrm{eff}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
with leverage score
\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
as in
Definition

2.2
. We have

\displaystyle\frac{m}{m+2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}\mathbf{I}_{n}\preceq\mathbf{D}\preceq\frac{m}{m+\rho_{\min}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}\mathbf{I}_{n},

in the sense of p.s.d. matrices, where we recall
\rho_{\max}=\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}/(\pi_{i}d_{\mathrm{eff}})
and
\rho_{\min}=\min_{1\leq i\leq n}\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}/(\pi_{i}d_{\mathrm{eff}})
.

Proof of
Lemma

D.3
.

For each
D_{ii}
,
i=1,\ldots,n
, it follows from its definition in
Theorem

3.1
that

\displaystyle D_{ii}=\frac{m\pi_{i}}{m\pi_{i}+_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{i}}\geq\frac{m\pi_{i}}{m\pi_{i}+D^{-1}_{\min}{}_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{i}},

where
D_{\min}=\min_{1\leq i\leq n}D_{ii}\leq 1
.
Without loss of generality, let
D_{\min}=D_{nn}
and use the fact that
m\geq C\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}\geq C\ell_{n}^{\mathbf{C}}/\pi_{n}=C_{n}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{n}/\pi_{n}
, we obtain a lower bound on
D_{nn}
as

\displaystyle D_{nn}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\pi_{n}}{m\pi_{n}+_{n}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{n}}\geq\frac{m\pi_{n}}{m\pi_{n}+D^{-1}_{nn}{}_{n}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{n}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m\pi_{n}}{m\pi_{n}+D^{-1}_{nn}C^{-1}m\pi_{n}}=\frac{C}{C+D^{-1}_{nn}},

so that
D_{nn}\geq\frac{C-1}{C}\equiv\Delta>1/2
, for some
C>2
.
Then, it follows that, for
i=1,\ldots,n
,

\displaystyle D_{ii}\geq\frac{m\pi_{i}}{m\pi_{i}+\Delta^{-1}_{i}{}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{i}}\geq\frac{m}{m+\Delta^{-1}_{i}{}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{i}/\pi_{i}}\geq\frac{m}{m+\Delta^{-1}\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}.

This together with
C>2
results in

\displaystyle D_{ii}\geq\frac{m}{m+2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle D_{ii}

\displaystyle=\frac{m}{m+_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{i}/\pi_{i}}\leq\frac{m}{m+_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}_{i}/\pi_{i}}\leq\frac{m}{m+\rho_{\min}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}.

Consequently, we have that
\frac{m}{m+2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}\mathbf{I}_{n}\preceq\mathbf{D}\preceq\frac{m}{m+\rho_{\min}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}\mathbf{I}_{n}
, this concludes the proof of
Lemma

D.3
.
∎

Appendix E
Proof of
Proposition

3.2

In this section, we start by presenting the RMT intuition for
Proposition

3.2
, and in particular how the
exact
leverage scores
\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}
comes into play in the analysis and debiasing, in
Section

E.1
.
The detailed proof of
Proposition

3.2
is then given in
Section

E.2
; and finally
we provide discussions and auxiliary results on
Proposition

3.2
in
Section

E.3
.

E.1
RMT Intuition on
Proposition

3.2

Here, we present the heuristic derivation of
Proposition

3.2
.
First, let us recall some notations from
Proposition

3.2
:
let
\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}=\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s}}/\sqrt{\pi_{i_{s}}}\mathbf{A}
as in
Appendix

C
,
for the ease of further use, let

\displaystyle\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}=\sum^{m}_{s=1}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}~\cdot~\frac{\mathbf{e}_{i_{s}}\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}},

for some
deterministic

F_{ii}
to be specified, and

\check{\mathbf{Q}}=(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}=\left(\frac{1}{m}\sum^{m}_{s=1}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{x}_{s}{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}_{s}+\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1},

and similarly
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}=(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{l\neq s}F_{i_{l}i_{l}}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathbf{x}_{l}^{\top}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
, for which we have

\frac{1}{m}\sum^{m}_{s=1}{\mathbb{E}}[F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{x}_{s}{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}_{s}]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}F_{ii}{}_{i}{}_{i}^{\top}.

Our objective here is to find
\check{\mathbf{Q}}
(and
F_{ii}
) such that
\|{\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]-\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|\simeq 0
, for
\mathbf{H}^{-1}=(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
.
This is

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]-\mathbf{H}^{-1}={\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}-{\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{A}]\mathbf{H}^{-1}\simeq 0.

Using the Sherman-Morrison formula in
Lemma

A.3
, we further ascertain that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i_{s}}\mathbf{e}_{i_{s}}^{\top}/\pi_{i_{s}}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}}{1+F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{e}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i_{s}}/m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}F_{ii}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}}{1+F_{ii}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}F_{ii}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}}{1+F_{ii}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\right],

where we observe that the
exact
leverage score
\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{e}_{i}=\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}\mathbf{a}_{i}=\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}
given
\mathbf{C}
as in
Definition

2.2
naturally appears in the denominator from this leave-one-out derivation.

This, together with the rank-one perturbation lemma in
Silverstein and Bai [1995, Lemma 2.6]
, gives that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}]\simeq{\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{F_{ii}\mathbf{e}_{i}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}}{1+F_{ii}\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}/m\pi_{i}}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1}={\mathbb{E}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1},

where we take the
debiasing factor

F_{ii}=m\pi_{i}/(m\pi_{i}-\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}})
such that
F_{ii}/(1+F_{ii}\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}/m\pi_{i})=1
.
This thus leads to

\displaystyle\check{\mathbf{S}}=\diag\left\{\sqrt{m/(m-\ell_{i_{s}}^{\mathbf{C}}/\pi_{i_{s}})}\right\}^{m}_{s=1}\cdot\mathbf{S},\quad i_{s}\in\{1,\ldots,n\}.

E.2
Detailed Proof of
Proposition

3.2

The proof of
Proposition

3.2
follows the same line of arguments as in that of
Theorem

3.1
.
In the following, we presented the proof for completeness.
We specifically highlight the difference in the proof of
Proposition

3.2
from that of
Theorem

3.1
, where
\mathbf{Q}
is replaced by
\check{\mathbf{Q}}
.

The proof of
Proposition

3.2
also comes in the following two steps:

1.

construct a high-probability event
\zeta
, as in (
20
); and

2.

conditional on the event
\zeta
, derive a bound for the spectral norm
\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}\|
using again the “leave-one-out” type analysis.

For the second term above, we have, for
m>2\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}
that

\displaystyle F_{i_{s}i_{s}}<\frac{m\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-2^{-1}m\pi_{i_{s}}}<2,~s=1,\ldots,m.

(34)

This yields that, for given
s\in\{1,\ldots,m\}
, there exists an index
j=j(s)\in\{1,2,3\}
such that
\check{\mathbf{Q}}\preceq\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\preceq 6\mathbf{H}^{-1}
holds on the event
\zeta_{j}
.

Denote
\check{\gamma}_{s}=1+\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}
, we then obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}-\mathbf{I}_{d}

\displaystyle=({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]-\mathbf{H}^{-1})\mathbf{H}={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}-\sum^{m}_{s=1}\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\right)\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right]\mathbf{H}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}-\sum^{m}_{s=1}\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\right)\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}_{s}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}-\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}}_{\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})]}_{\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\left(1-\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{s}}\right)\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\right]}_{\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}},

which leads to

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|=\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|.

(35)

We first bound the first term
\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|
in (
35
).
Adapting the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}-\mathbf{Q}]
and
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1/2}\|
in the proof of
Theorem

3.1
in
Section

D.2
, and using (
34
) along with the fact that
\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\preceq 6\mathbf{I}_{d}
when conditioned on
\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}-\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\preceq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{s}m}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\right]\preceq\frac{4}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{H}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}],

(36)

and

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}-\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\frac{1}{m}\right).

Next, we bound the second term
\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|
in (
35
).
Note that
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}]=d_{\mathrm{eff}}
and

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s})^{2}]=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\pi_{j}\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j})^{2}}{\pi^{2}_{j}}=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\frac{(_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j})^{2}}{\pi_{j}}\leq\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}.

Then, using Chebyshev’s inequality, we have, for
x\geq 2d_{\mathrm{eff}}
that

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})\leq\frac{\rho_{\max}d^{2}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{x^{2}}.

Analogous to the bound on
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{Q}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{A}-\tilde{D}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})]
and
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1/2}\|
in
Section

D.2
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})]=-\frac{1}{1-\delta_{3}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s})\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}],

and

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 12\delta_{3}+12{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}[\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}\neg\zeta_{3}]=12\delta_{3}+12\int^{\infty}_{0}\Pr(\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{3}}\geq x~|~\zeta^{\prime})dx

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Now, we move on to bound the last term
\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|
in (
35
).
Considering (
34
), it follows that

\displaystyle\left|1-\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{s}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}+_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}\right|=F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|=2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-_{i_{s}}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|.

(37)

Following the bound on
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1/2}\|
in
Section

D.2
, and recalling (
34
), we further have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq\sup\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1,\|\mathbf{v}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\left|1-\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{s}}\right|\left|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{v}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}74\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{j}-_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\right]\leq\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}74\left|\frac{{}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{j}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{j}]}{m\pi_{j}}\right|\right]}_{\check{M}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\frac{74\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{j}]-_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}\right|}{m\pi_{j}}}_{\check{M}_{2}}.

(38)

Subsequently, like the bounds for
M_{1}
and
M_{2}
in
Section

D.2
, and using (
34
), we obtain the following bounds for
\check{M}_{1}
and
\check{M}_{2}
:

\displaystyle\check{M}_{1}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\check{M}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\frac{74_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{-1}_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}(\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{\prime}}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta})[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}-\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|+\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Combining the above, we conclude that

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{I}_{d}-\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}]\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right),

and thus complete the proof of
Proposition

3.2
.

\Box

E.3
Discussions on
Proposition

3.2
and Auxiliary Results

Here, we present some auxiliary results in addition to the fine-grained debiasing results in
Proposition

3.2
.
Precisely, we show in
Section

E.3.1
that by substituting exact leverage scores with some (computationally efficient) approximations leads to a controlled inversion bias.
We then provide the proof of
Corollary

3.4
in
Section

E.3.2
.
A “counter-example” to
Dereziński et al. [2021b, Theorem 10]
that uses scalar debiasing to achieve zero inversion bias in the case of
exact
leverage score sampling is given in
Section

E.3.3
.
Finally, we establish the proof of
Corollary

3.7
in
Section

E.3.4
.

E.3.1
Discussions on Debiasing
\check{\mathbf{S}}
Using Approximate Leverage Scores

Corollary E.1

(Fine-grained debiasing
\check{\mathbf{S}}
using approximate leverage scores)
.

Under the setting and notations of
Theorem

3.1
, if one uses approximate leverage scores
\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
in the debiasing matrix, instead of the exact leverage scores
\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
in (
5
) in
Proposition

3.2
, that is

\check{\mathbf{S}}=\diag\left\{\sqrt{m/(m-\check{\ell}_{i_{s}}^{\mathbf{C}}/\pi_{i_{s}})}\right\}^{m}_{s=1}\cdot\mathbf{S},\quad(1-\omega)\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}\leq\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\leq(1+\omega)\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}},\quad\omega\in[0,1].

Then, there exists
C>0
independent of
n,d_{\mathrm{eff}}
such that for
m\geq C\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}(\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/\delta)+\max\left\{\omega/\epsilon,(\log n)^{2/3}/(\epsilon\log(\log n))^{2/3}\right\})
with
\delta\leq m^{-3}
,
(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
is an
(\epsilon,\delta)
-unbiased estimator of
(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
.

Proof of
Corollary

E.1
.

The proof of
Corollary

E.1
largely follows the proof of
Proposition

3.2
, and is presented here for completeness.
In the following, we emphasize the difference from the proof of
Proposition

3.2
, particularly regarding the matrix
\check{\mathbf{S}}
, which is constructed using the approximate leverage scores denoted by
\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
as

\displaystyle\check{\mathbf{S}}=\diag\left\{\sqrt{m/(m-\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}/\pi_{i_{s}}})\right\}^{m}_{s=1}\cdot\mathbf{S},\quad i_{s}\in\{1,\ldots,n\}.

This implies, in the proof of
Proposition

3.2
that

\displaystyle F_{i_{s}i_{s}}=\frac{m\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-\check{\ell}_{i_{s}}^{\mathbf{C}}}.

(39)

Note that in this setting, we have, for
\omega\in[0,1]
that
|\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}|\leq\omega\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
and
\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\leq(1+\omega)\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\leq 2\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
.
As such, for
m\geq C\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}\geq C\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}/\pi_{i_{s}}
with
C>4
, we have

\displaystyle F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\leq\frac{m\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-2\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}}<2.

Thus, adapting the proof of
Proposition

3.2
, we get the same bounds on
\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|
and
\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|
as in
Section

E.2
, that is

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\frac{1}{m}\right),~\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Next, we establish a bound for the term
\|\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}\|
in the setting of
Corollary

E.1
.
Using (
39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|1-\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{s}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}+_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{(1-C^{-1})m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|+2\left|\frac{\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}-\check{\ell}^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|+\frac{2\omega\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}.

This, together with (
38
) yields that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\frac{\omega\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}+\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Putting these bounds together, we conclude the proof of
Corollary

E.1
.
∎

E.3.2
Proof of
Corollary

3.4

The proof
Corollary

3.4
largely mirrors that of
Proposition

3.2
, and is included here for thoroughness.
For
m>Cd_{\mathrm{eff}}
with
C>2
, we have

\displaystyle F_{i_{s}i_{s}}=\frac{m}{m-d_{\mathrm{eff}}}=\frac{m\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i_{s}}}\leq\frac{m\pi_{i_{s}}}{(1-C^{-1})m\pi_{i_{s}}}<2.

Then, recalling
\pi_{i}\in[\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}/(d_{\mathrm{eff}}\rho_{\max}),\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}/(d_{\mathrm{eff}}\rho_{\min})]
such that
|\pi_{i}-\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}/d_{\mathrm{eff}}|\leq\epsilon_{\rho}\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}/d_{\mathrm{eff}}
with
\epsilon_{\rho}=\max\{(\rho_{\min}^{-1}-1),(1-\rho_{\max}^{-1})\}
, and (
37
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\left|1-\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{s}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i_{s}}+_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i_{s}}}{(1-C^{-1})m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|+2\left|\frac{\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|+2d_{\mathrm{eff}}\left|\frac{\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}/d_{\mathrm{eff}}-\pi_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|+2\epsilon_{\rho}d_{\mathrm{eff}}\frac{\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}/d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\frac{{}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}{}_{i_{s}}-\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{s}}}{m\pi_{i_{s}}}\right|+\frac{2\epsilon_{\rho}\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}.

This, combined with the bound in (
38
), gives that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{3}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\rho}\rho_{\max}d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m}+\frac{\log n}{\log(\log n)}\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Recall the proof of
Proposition

3.2
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{1}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\frac{1}{m}\right),~\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}\check{\mathbf{Z}}_{2}\mathbf{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho_{\max}^{3}d^{3}_{\mathrm{eff}}}{m^{3}}}\right).

Putting these together, we conclude the proof of
Corollary

3.4
.

In particular, taking
\rho_{\max}=3/2
and
\rho_{\min}=1/2
, we have
\epsilon_{\rho}=1
and thus an inversion bias of order
O(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/m)
.

\Box

E.3.3
A “Counterexample” to
Dereziński et al. [2021b, Theorem 10]

Corollary E.2

(“Counterexample” to the lower bound in
Dereziński et al. [2021b, Theorem 10]
using exact leverage sampling)
.

For any
n\geq 2d\geq 4
, there exists
\mathbf{A}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times d}
, an
exact
leverage score sampling matrix
\mathbf{S}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
in
Definition

2.2
and a high probability event
\zeta
(that ensures the invertibility of
\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}
), such that when conditioned on
\zeta
,
(\gamma\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
is an unbiased estimator of
(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
for
\gamma=\frac{m}{d}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[1/b]
,
b
distributed as
{\rm Binomial}(m,1/d)
, and
\mathbf{C}=\mathbf{0}_{d}
.

Proof of
Corollary

E.2
.

We begin by recalling the setup of the matrix
\mathbf{A}
in
Dereziński et al. [2021b, Theorem 10]
.
Without loss of generality, assume
n=2d
(otherwise, we pad
\mathbf{A}
with zeros). The matrix
\mathbf{A}
consists of
n=2d
scaled standard basis vectors, where consecutive rows are defined as
{}^{\top}_{2(i-1)+1}=^{\top}_{2(i-1)+2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i}
for
i\geq 2
, and the first two rows are
{}^{\top}_{1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{4}}\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{1}
,
{}^{\top}_{2}=\frac{3}{\sqrt{4}}\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{1}
.
This is

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{4}}&0&\dots&0\\
\frac{3}{\sqrt{4}}&0&\dots&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}&\dots&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}&\dots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
0&0&\dots&\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\
0&0&\dots&\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{bmatrix}.

In this case we have
\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{I}_{d}
and
\mathbf{C}=\mathbf{0}_{d}
, so that the leverage scores satisfy
\ell_{1}^{\mathbf{C}}=\frac{1}{4}
,
\ell_{2}^{\mathbf{C}}=\frac{3}{4}
, and
\ell_{2(i-1)+1}^{\mathbf{C}}=\ell_{2(i-1)+2}^{\mathbf{C}}=\frac{1}{2}
for
i\geq 2
.
The leverage score sampling distribution
\{\pi_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
as in
Definition

2.2
, used to construct the sampling matrix
\mathbf{S}
of size
m\geq d
, is thus given by

\displaystyle\pi_{i}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{4d},&\text{for }i=1,\\
\frac{3}{4d},&\text{for }i=2,\\
\frac{1}{2d},&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

For any
\gamma>0
, the matrix
\gamma\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}
is diagonal, with diagonal entries given by

\displaystyle\left[\gamma\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\right]_{ii}=\frac{\gamma db_{i}}{m},~\mbox{for}~i=1,\ldots,d,

where
b_{i}
’s are all identically (but not independently) distributed as
{\rm Binomial}(m,1/d)
.
Then, conditioning on
any
high probability event
\zeta
that ensures the invertibility of
\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}
, it follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\left[\left(\gamma\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\right)^{-1}\right]_{ii}\right]=\frac{m}{\gamma d}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{1}{b_{i}}\right],~\mbox{for}~i=1,\ldots,d,

so that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\left(\gamma\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{S}^{\top}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{A}\right)^{-1}\right]=\left(\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\right)^{-1}

with
\gamma=\frac{m}{d}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[1/b\right]
, where
b
is any one of the aforementioned
\{b_{i}\}^{d}_{i=1}
.
This completes the proof of
Corollary

E.2
.
∎

E.3.4
Proof of
Corollary

3.7

The proof of
Corollary

3.7
comes in the following two steps:

1.

construct two
independent
high probability events:
\zeta_{\omega}
on which the randomized Walsh–Hadamard transform
\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}
of
\mathbf{A}
as in
Definition

3.6
has approximately uniform leverages scores; and
\zeta
as in (
20
) to subspace embedding, but for
\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{s}=\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s}}\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n\pi_{i_{s}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
the
i_{s}^{th}
row of
\tilde{\mathbf{A}}_{\rm SRHT}
; and

2.

conditioned on the event
\zeta\bigcap\zeta_{\omega}
, apply the bounds in
Section

E.3.2
to obtain inversion bias for
(\frac{m}{m-d_{\mathrm{eff}}}\tilde{\mathbf{A}}_{\rm SRHT}^{\top}\tilde{\mathbf{A}}_{\rm SRHT}+\mathbf{C})^{-1}
, where
d_{\mathrm{eff}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}
is the effective dimension of
\mathbf{A}
given
\mathbf{C}
.

We start by recalling some notations from (the proof of)
Proposition

3.2
.
Denote
\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}}=\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}
with
\mathbf{H}=\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{C}
and let
\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n})=\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}}\|^{2}/n
be the
i^{th}
leverage score of
\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n}
.
Define the following event
\zeta_{\omega}
:

\displaystyle\zeta_{\omega}:\left|\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}(\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n})-\frac{d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{n}\right|\leq\omega,\quad\forall 1\leq i\leq n.

Note that this is equivalent to, for uniform sampling with
\pi_{i}=1/n
that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}(\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n})-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right|\leq\frac{n}{m}\omega,\quad\forall 1\leq i\leq n.

Next, we verify that the event
\zeta_{\omega}
holds with a controlled probability.
Precisely,
some
C>0
, it follows from
Lemma

E.4
below and
\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}^{2}=d_{\mathrm{eff}}
that

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left|\ell_{i}^{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n})-\frac{d_{\mathrm{eff}}}{n}\right|\geq\omega,1\leq i\leq n\right)\leq\frac{\delta}{2},

(40)

with
\omega\geq\max\{C\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{eff}}\log(n/\delta)}/n,C\log(n/\delta)/n\}
, which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\ell^{\mathbf{C}}_{i}(\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{A}/\sqrt{n})-d_{\mathrm{eff}}\pi_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right|\leq\max\left\{\frac{C\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{eff}}\log(n/\delta)}}{m},\frac{C\log(n/\delta)}{m}\right\},~\text{for}~1\leq i\leq n.

Further recall from
Lacotte et al. [2021, Lemma 1]
that a sampling size
